# Control-to-code map

Generated by `pnpm handbook` from the repository and [modules.yaml](../modules.yaml); do not edit.

Each control of the register ([compliance/controls/](../../../compliance/controls/)) with the code that carries it
and the test files that prove it, as the register records them. The tests carry the control ID in their names, for
example `[DI-01]`. Modules are the [code map](../code-map.md) modules whose files the control names.

246 controls: 13 IMPLEMENTED, 122 SPECIFIED, 111 VERIFIED.

## DI-01

**Record-type, official-record and raw-data register.** [DI-01.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/DI-01.yaml). Target phase 1; editions tablets, injectables; SPECIFIED.

- Modules: [kernel-records](../code-map.md#kernel-records), [kernel-authorization](../code-map.md#kernel-authorization)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/records/register.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/records/register.ts)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0400_records_register.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0400_records_register.sql)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0720_authorization_signatures.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0720_authorization_signatures.sql)
- Guard: records.assert_classification_valid / records.classification_problems: raw-data floor for validators (MBR approval in Phase 2)
- Guard: records.approve_record_type: approver is a person other than the author; only newer versions become current
- Guard: records.approve_record_type accepts only the approver's own valid RECORD_TYPE_APPROVED signature of that version (esig.assert_signature on record_type_version &lt;code&gt;:&lt;version&gt;; EBMR-REC-017); the author cannot sign it (INV-03)
- Tests: [packages/kernel/test/records/register.test.ts](../../../packages/kernel/test/records/register.test.ts)

- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/flow/canonical.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/flow/canonical.ts) (content hash)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/validator](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/validator)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/simulator](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/simulator) (simulate, evidence)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0120_mbr_evidence.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0120_mbr_evidence.sql) (MBR life cycle: evidence stored with the exact content hash, result decided by the database, voided by any content edit)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0130_mbr_guards.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0130_mbr_guards.sql) (guards validator_passed, evidence_complete)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/1830_evidence_sandbox_optional.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/1830_evidence_sandbox_optional.sql) (owner decision 0026 O-2: the evidence is the validator report, the simulation and the test vectors on the exact content; a sandbox run is optional, and one recorded that did not pass keeps the evidence incomplete)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0160_market_authorisation.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0160_market_authorisation.sql) (no market authorisation without complete verification evidence)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/lifecycle/verification.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/lifecycle/verification.ts) (verification port over the validator and simulator)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0150_designer.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0150_designer.sql) (tab.reference_document DMS stand-in: insert-only, audited, hash recomputed; tab.designer_justification)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/designer/adapters](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/designer/adapters) (ReferencePort over tab.reference_document; MarketMinimaPort over market.resolve, failing closed)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/designer/simulation.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/designer/simulation.ts) and simulate-worker.ts (simulation of the saved content in a worker thread with a timeout; no writes)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/designer/index.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/designer/index.ts) (startVerification: lifecycle.validateAndStartVerification; evidence views valid only for the current content hash)
- Code: [apps/tablets-web/src/features/designer/model/ops.ts](../../../apps/tablets-web/src/features/designer/model/ops.ts) (an edit that would bring text no record can hold into the MBR content is refused, naming the field, so the designer never hashes such content; review F31 round 2)
- Guard: buildVerificationEvidence / verifyMbr: validator report, warning justifications and simulation report bound to the content hash
- Guard: evidenceHolds: only unaltered VERIFIED evidence of exactly this content counts (for the MBR life-cycle gate)
- Guard: GMP-SYSTEMS (computerised system and recipe version named), GMP-PAT (PAT acceptance criteria), GMP-REFERENCES (pinned and effective), FLOW-LIBRARY (pinned element hash)
- Guard: deltaVerification: value-only changes reuse structural evidence and rerun the changed items
- Guard: mbr_version.validator_passed (DRAFT -&gt; IN_VERIFICATION) and mbr_version.evidence_complete (SUBMIT, APPROVE): validator, simulation, author-confirmed test vectors and a sandbox run (or delta verification) recorded for the exact content hash
- Guard: recipe.activate_market_authorisation: QA cannot make a version CURRENT for a market without a complete verification record (EBMR-RCP-087)

- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0344_sandbox_free_text_identifiers.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0344_sandbox_free_text_identifiers.sql) (every text and JSON member a test run records, and every reason and business entry of its audit trail, read with kernel 1030 at commit)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0345_start_data_and_status_equipment.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0345_start_data_and_status_equipment.sql) (no accepted start data in a test run; start-data authoriser and equipment status bound)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0630_sandbox_lot_material_name.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0630_sandbox_lot_material_name.sql) (a test lot's material name under sandbox.guard_free_text)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/1050_sandbox_identifier_as_shown.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/1050_sandbox_identifier_as_shown.sql) (the identifier rule reads a text as it shows: what shows nothing and the combining marks removed, look-alikes read through sandbox.look_alike, a text that can display in another order read in every arrangement of its runs)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0349_sandbox_reading_as_shown.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0349_sandbox_reading_as_shown.sql) (bound words and a refusal's path read in the same forms)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/1060_sandbox_look_alike_names.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/1060_sandbox_look_alike_names.sql) (letters and digits that Unicode names, with no decomposition, read as named)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/1070_sandbox_separator_look_alikes.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/1070_sandbox_separator_look_alikes.sql) (the look-alikes of the separators read as a class: the UTS #39 class of U+30FC and the classes of letters read by name, a letter drawn with a dot or an apostrophe, dashes and solidi by name and drawing, compatibility forms newer than PostgreSQL 16's NFKC, spaces narrower than the thin space)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/1080_sandbox_full_stop_look_alikes.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/1080_sandbox_full_stop_look_alikes.sql) (the look-alikes of the full stop read as kernel 1070 reads those of the dash and the solidus)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/1090_sandbox_no_advance.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/1090_sandbox_no_advance.sql) (what the E2E browser lays out with no advance read as nothing: every spacing combining mark, U+FFFC and the other characters a DOM-layout scan of Chromium 141 found)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/1101_sandbox_letter_shapes.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/1101_sandbox_letter_shapes.sql) (letters and digits read as Chromium 141 draws them: a drawing scan against the ASCII letters and digits, alone and with a combining mark)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/1102_sandbox_letter_shapes_by_weight.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/1102_sandbox_letter_shapes_by_weight.sql) (letters and digits read as Chromium 141 draws them at the weights the product draws free text in, 400, 600 and 700, with a complete comparison)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/1120_sandbox_digit_words_ascii.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/1120_sandbox_digit_words_ascii.sql) (in a sandbox a word that holds a digit is written with ASCII letters, digits, '/', '.' and '-' only, as stored and in every reading: sandbox.text_words, sandbox.non_ascii_digit_words, read whole through sandbox.identifier_words and sandbox.real_identifier_parts by every guard of the rule; decision F12.14)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0350_sandbox_digit_words_bound.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0350_sandbox_digit_words_bound.sql) (such a word is bound in a test run only as its MBR or header holds it whole; its refusal says to write ASCII digits and a space)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/1130_sandbox_reordered_digit_words.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/1130_sandbox_reordered_digit_words.sql) (a text that can display in another order is read in every arrangement of its runs, the runs of characters outside ASCII included, so a word with a digit is plain ASCII also as a reordered line shows it; decision F12.15)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/1140_sandbox_reordering_ranges.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/1140_sandbox_reordering_ranges.sql) (the characters that can make a text display in another order are every code point of the ranges reserved for right-to-left scripts and the bidi controls and marks, so the class holds the bidirectional data of the E2E browser, Chromium 141 with ICU 74, and not of Unicode 18.0.0 only; decision F12.16)
- Guard: kernel.installation_kind(): GMP or SANDBOX, set once at provisioning; refuses a missing marker or one that disagrees with the database name (EBMR-SBX-001, EBMR-SBX-004)
- Guard: sandbox.assert_gmp_installation(action) on GMP-issuing paths (EBMR-SBX-010); sandbox.assert_sandbox_installation(action) on sandbox-only features (EBMR-SBX-011)
- Guard: sandbox.assert_identifier_allowed(kind, value) and trigger sandbox.guard_identifier: GMP refuses TEST- identifiers (EBMR-SBX-021), the sandbox refuses anything else (EBMR-SBX-022)
- Guard: sandbox.assert_batch_issue_allowed(market states): GMP batches only from CURRENT or BRIDGING versions (EBMR-SBX-012)
- Guard: assertInstallationKind at process start-up (EBMR-SBX-007); assertGmpOrigin on anything imported into GMP (EBMR-SBX-013)
- Guard: recipe.execution_actor: every execution writer refuses a GMP installation (EBMR-SBX-011) and administrators
- Guard: recipe.import_test_package: package and content hashes recomputed in SQL, file name = package hash, GMP source stamp without watermark (EBMR-EXE-016..019)
- Guard: recipe.begin_issue / issue_execution: TEST-B&lt;yy&gt;-&lt;nnnn&gt; from a sequence, only the number allocated in this transaction (EBMR-EXE-012); guard trigger on recipe.batch_execution
- Guard: defineBatch(installation GMP) refuses SANDBOX_ONLY block versions (ENG-DEF-SANDBOX-ONLY); test runs execute only SANDBOX definitions (EBMR-EXE-064)
- Guard: sandbox.guard_identifier on market.stub_batch.batch_number, market.stub_material_lot.code and market.stub_pack.code (kernel 1030; EBMR-SBX-021 in GMP, EBMR-SBX-022 in the sandbox); sandbox.guard_free_text: outside a GMP installation no word the identifier rule reads as a real batch or lot number in the named free-text columns (kernel 1030, on the stubs free text; EBMR-SBX-024)
- Guard: recipe-core 0344: deferred constraint triggers zzz_sandbox_free_text on every execution table the application role writes and on kernel.audit_event (a run's chains: reason and business entries; a device reading's chain: reason): no word the identifier rule reads as a real batch, lot or container number, in any string or member name at any depth, besides the words the record binds (its batch number, site, room, MBR content, the engine's own names) (EBMR-EXE-147)
- Guard: recipe-core 0345: an accepted START_STEP records no start data; start data names its authoriser by the id of a person of this installation; an equipment status at a step listing no equipment for a status check names equipment the MBR binds where the step runs (EBMR-EXE-126)
- Guard: tablets 0630: tab.sandbox_lot.material_name under sandbox.guard_free_text (EBMR-SBX-024)

## DI-10

**Version-in-force stamping (executed-context snapshot).** [DI-10.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/DI-10.yaml). Target phase 4; editions tablets, injectables; VERIFIED.

- Modules: [rc-blocks](../code-map.md#rc-blocks), [rc-library](../code-map.md#rc-library), [rc-execution](../code-map.md#rc-execution), [ts-blocks](../code-map.md#ts-blocks), [ts-review](../code-map.md#ts-review), [ts-release](../code-map.md#ts-release), [ts-execution](../code-map.md#ts-execution)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/definition.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/definition.ts), [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/batch.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/batch.ts) (pinned MBR version at issue)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/blocks](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/blocks) (contract, registry: versioned block types type@version)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/blocks/generic/recording.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/blocks/generic/recording.ts) (G-03)
- Guard: defineBatch compiles one pinned MBR version (content hash) and refuses a phase whose block type@version has no registered execution handler (ENG-DEF-BLOCK) or whose configuration is outside that version's schema (ENG-DEF-CONFIG)
- Guard: Issue (G-01) pins the MBR content hash in the batch state; header and master-data inputs are typed and stored with their source
- Guard: Each data point stores its limit evaluation at recording, and each step instance the targets it showed, so later changes cannot alter them
- Guard: G-03: the SOP code, version and hash pinned by the MBR are written into the acknowledgement entry; another version is refused (G03-SOP-VERSION); an SOP the MBR does not reference cannot be executed (G03-SOP-REF)

## DI-13

**Security baseline, confidentiality and non-repudiation evidence.** [DI-13.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/DI-13.yaml). Target phase 1; editions tablets, injectables; SPECIFIED.

- Modules: [kernel-esig](../code-map.md#kernel-esig), [kernel-continuity](../code-map.md#kernel-continuity), [kernel-integration](../code-map.md#kernel-integration), [kernel-installation](../code-map.md#kernel-installation), [rc-formula](../code-map.md#rc-formula), [rc-execution](../code-map.md#rc-execution), [ts-session](../code-map.md#ts-session), [ts-esig](../code-map.md#ts-esig), [ts-master-data](../code-map.md#ts-master-data), [ts-transfers](../code-map.md#ts-transfers), [ts-core](../code-map.md#ts-core), [tablets-web](../code-map.md#tablets-web), [tools-e2e](../code-map.md#tools-e2e), [tools-security](../code-map.md#tools-security)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/continuity/security-baseline.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/continuity/security-baseline.ts) (assertProductionConfig, assertEncryptedDatabaseSession, checkPgHba, checkPostgresqlConf)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/continuity/hardening.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/continuity/hardening.ts)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/continuity/backup-drill.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/continuity/backup-drill.ts) (AES-256-GCM encryption of backups at rest)
- Code: [ops/postgres/pg_hba.conf.template](../../../ops/postgres/pg_hba.conf.template)
- Code: [ops/postgres/postgresql.tls.conf.template](../../../ops/postgres/postgresql.tls.conf.template)
- Code: [docs/security/security-baseline.md](../../security/security-baseline.md)
- Code: [docs/security/threat-model.md](../../security/threat-model.md)
- Code: [docs/security/asvs-l2-review.md](../../security/asvs-l2-review.md)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0240_esig_integrity.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0240_esig_integrity.sql) (esig.evidence_package: non-repudiation evidence package of a signature)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/esig/integrity.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/esig/integrity.ts) (evidencePackage with seal verification against the trusted key ring)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0550_session_terminal_key.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0550_session_terminal_key.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-web/src/api/page-binding.ts](../../../apps/tablets-web/src/api/page-binding.ts)
- Guard: continuity.assert_device_hardened (enrolment guard for SHARED and GRADE_A_B devices; wiring into device enrolment by the lead)
- Guard: assertProductionConfig refuses non-TLS database URLs, non-HTTPS servers, plain-HTTP serving, weak cookies and plain-text integration endpoints
- Guard: assertEncryptedDatabaseSession refuses a database session that pg_stat_ssl reports as not encrypted
- Guard: esig.evidence_package: signature row, stored manifestation, authentication event (components, method, terminal, time; no secret, verifier or badge UID), identity-verification record, signer as at signing, authority and segregation evaluations, delegation, invalidation, seal with payload check, integrity problems and the audit chains of the record and its signatures with their verification
- Guard: installSecurity: an API request that carries this installation's session or CSRF cookie more than once is served as nobody (401 COOKIE_DUPLICATED) and the copies other pages set are removed; helmet's headers are set before the gate, and Fastify's framework errors carry them too (review F41, F43)
- Guard: sign-out-mark.ts: the mark of an unconfirmed sign-out is kept only in site storage and this origin's broadcast channel, never in a cookie other ports of the host can write (review F42)
- Guard: pageBindingRefusal (bind.ts) on tab.resolve_session's terminal key (tablets 0550): a request whose page names its terminal key or the person its session started with is served as nobody when the cookie's session was opened on another terminal (401 SESSION_OTHER_TERMINAL) or is another person's (401 SESSION_CHANGED); the SPA names both on every request (page-binding.ts, the person kept per tab) and then shows the sign-in form or says that its sign-in changed, never working in that session (review F41, round 4; residuals in decision F41.1)
- Tests: [apps/tablets-server/test/esig/read-authorisation.test.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/test/esig/read-authorisation.test.ts), - Code: [apps/tablets-web/src/features/access/controller.tsx](../../../apps/tablets-web/src/features/access/controller.tsx) (BadgeTapPort, switch offers, lock overlay, session poll)
- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/machines/kiosk-lock.ts](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/machines/kiosk-lock.ts)
- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/identity/LockOverlay.tsx](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/identity/LockOverlay.tsx)
- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/identity/BadgePrompt.tsx](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/identity/BadgePrompt.tsx)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0550_session_terminal_key.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0550_session_terminal_key.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-web/src/api/page-binding.ts](../../../apps/tablets-web/src/api/page-binding.ts)
- Guard: iam.check_static_segregation (triggers on iam.role_grant and iam.role_permission): generic and READONLY_DISPLAY accounts hold READ permissions only; SERVICE and INTEGRATION accounts never hold SIGN or ACKNOWLEDGE permissions
- Guard: kernel.validate_actor_context: generic and read-only display accounts can never start a command (EBMR-IAM-106); sources SYSTEM and INTEGRATION only for service accounts, UI and API only for people
- Guard: iam.authn_resolve: service keys only for service accounts on SERVER devices, interactive methods never for service accounts, read-only accounts never authenticate to act or sign (alerted)
- Guard: INV-40: every command on a SHARED or GRADE_A_B device consumes a fresh authentication event of its own person made on that device (kernel.validate_actor_context, iam.use_auth_event_for_command)
- Guard: pageBindingRefusal (bind.ts) on tab.resolve_session's terminal key (tablets 0550): a request whose page names its terminal key or the person its session started with is served as nobody when the cookie's session was opened on another terminal (401 SESSION_OTHER_TERMINAL) or is another person's (401 SESSION_CHANGED); the SPA names both on every request (page-binding.ts, the person kept per tab) and then shows the sign-in form or says that its sign-in changed, never working in that session (review F41, round 4; residuals in decision F41.1)

## DI-15

**Identity verification, credential issuance and permanent identifier uniqueness.** [DI-15.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/DI-15.yaml). Target phase 1; editions tablets, injectables; SPECIFIED.

- Modules: [kernel-iam](../code-map.md#kernel-iam), [kernel-installation](../code-map.md#kernel-installation), [rc-lifecycle](../code-map.md#rc-lifecycle), [ts-session](../code-map.md#ts-session), [ts-execution](../code-map.md#ts-execution), [ts-administration](../code-map.md#ts-administration), [ts-core](../code-map.md#ts-core), [test-kit](../code-map.md#test-kit), [tools-e2e](../code-map.md#tools-e2e)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/identity/authentication.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/identity/authentication.ts)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/identity/secrets.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/identity/secrets.ts)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/identity/admin.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/identity/admin.ts)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0110_iam_persons_accounts.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0110_iam_persons_accounts.sql)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0150_iam_credentials_badges.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0150_iam_credentials_badges.sql)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0160_iam_authentication_sessions.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0160_iam_authentication_sessions.sql)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0170_iam_signing_readiness.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0170_iam_signing_readiness.sql)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0990_shared_terminal_secret_change_window.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0990_shared_terminal_secret_change_window.sql)
- Guard: iam.account_guard: activation needs an identity verification recorded for the current employment period (EBMR-IAM-013); nobody verifies their own identity
- Guard: iam.issue_credential and iam.badge_guard: no secret, PIN or badge before identity verification
- Guard: initial, admin-reset and bootstrap secrets are single-use, expire (credential.initial_secret_valid_hours) and yield only a restricted CREDENTIAL_CHANGE authentication event
- Guard: iam.can_sign / iam.assert_can_sign: no signature while a secret must be changed
- Guard: iam.consume_auth_event: the authentication event must belong to the signer (no proxy signing, EBMR-IAM-093)
- Guard: iam.assert_us_esig_certification: 21 CFR 11.100(c) letter per legal entity; BLOCK (outline, default) or FLAG (catalog) by policy
- Guard: iam.change_event_check (kernel 0990): on a SHARED or GRADE_A_B terminal a secret change counts only within that class's attribution window (attribution.freshness_seconds.&lt;class&gt;, EBMR-IAM-092); elsewhere within authn.event_max_age_seconds
- Guard: identity pinProblems: a new PIN longer than MAX_PIN_LENGTH (12, what every kiosk PIN pad takes) is refused before hashing (EBMR-IAM-055)

## DI-16

**Access lifecycle: recorded grants, point-in-time access lists, recertification, prompt removal.** [DI-16.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/DI-16.yaml). Target phase 1; editions tablets, injectables; SPECIFIED.

- Modules: [kernel-audit](../code-map.md#kernel-audit), [kernel-iam](../code-map.md#kernel-iam), [kernel-installation](../code-map.md#kernel-installation), [ts-session](../code-map.md#ts-session), [ts-audit](../code-map.md#ts-audit), [ts-devices](../code-map.md#ts-devices), [ts-administration](../code-map.md#ts-administration), [ts-core](../code-map.md#ts-core), [tools-e2e](../code-map.md#tools-e2e)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/identity/admin.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/identity/admin.ts)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0120_iam_roles_grants.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0120_iam_roles_grants.sql)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0180_iam_lifecycle.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0180_iam_lifecycle.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0400_session_role_withdrawn.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0400_session_role_withdrawn.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0480_bind_session_needs_live_role.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0480_bind_session_needs_live_role.sql)
- Guard: iam.role_grant_guard: requester, approver and grantee are three different persons; reason recorded; request, approval and revocation times from the database clock
- Guard: iam.access_list(at): point-in-time list of persons, accounts, roles, permissions and scopes with the access-request record behind each row
- Guard: iam.revoke_role_grant: effective at the revocation instant for commands and authority evaluations
- Guard: iam.recertify_account and iam.recertification_due: reviewer, outcome, last activity and next due date; a REVOKED outcome revokes at once
- Guard: iam.deactivate_account and iam.process_leaver: disable, revoke grants, end sessions, revoke credentials and badges in one transaction
- Guard: tab.resolve_session (tablets 0400): a session whose declared role is no longer granted now (revoked or past valid_to), or whose account holds no live role, resolves no role (state ROLE_WITHDRAWN); the server ends it at its next request (tab.end_withdrawn_session, ADMIN_TERMINATED)
- Guard: tab.keep_session_alive and tab.unlock_session (tablets 0400) refuse such a session (EBMR-TAB-017); the application role has no EXECUTE on iam.keep_session_alive and iam.unlock_session
- Guard: tab.bind_session (tablets 0480): with no role named, a session whose account holds no role that is valid now is never bound to a cookie (EBMR-TAB-021); the server refuses such a login first (403 NO_ROLE)

- Guard: iam.evaluate_authority(person, account, permission, scope, at): a role grant covering the scope plus every required qualification at that instant; every evaluation stored with its inputs
- Guard: iam.require_permission: refuses with EBMR-IAM-040; withGuardedCommand stores the refusal again outside the rolled-back command
- Guard: iam.assert_segregated and iam.evaluate_segregation: dynamic segregation evaluated on the person, every evaluation stored
- Guard: iam.record_entry_persons and iam.chain_persons: persons involved in a record or batch from the audit trail (stand-in until B2 signatures and the Phase 4 involvement set)
- Guard: iam.delegation_valid: delegation checked at the moment of action; approver is neither delegate nor delegator; no re-delegation
- Guard: every function of market, records, continuity and the site batch-number register that the application may call and that can write calls iam.require_permission before its first write (EBMR-IAM-040), in the object's site/product scope, installation-wide ({}) for installation objects, or at the actor's location (session site and room) for point-of-work actions; the stub scope tables check MASTERDATA.CHANGE in a statement trigger
- Guard: iam.platform_authority: the platform AUTHORITY hook over iam.evaluate_authority, for the validated actor in its declared role at its location; platform names map to catalogue codes (iam.platform_permission_code)
- Guard: permission-to-role map in 0700_authorization_catalogue.sql (least privilege; catalogue rows attributed to the SOFTWARE_RELEASE identity in the audit trail)
- Guard: approval signature types name the approving permissions (RECORD_TYPE_APPROVED, RECORD_DISPOSAL_APPROVED, DI_INVESTIGATION_CLOSED, RESTORE_APPROVED), so esig evaluates the same permission at signing
- Guard: tab.record_read_problem and tab.signing_preview_problem (tablets 0410): a session reads a RECORD-class record with RECORD.READ, a GOVERNANCE-class one (investigations, disposals, restores, any type not named) only in a governance or audit role, never an investigation about itself (since tablets 0530 answered as one that is not there), and previews content only with a meaning its role signs (EBMR-TAB-018, EBMR-TAB-019, EBMR-TAB-020)
- Guard: tab.audit_chains, tab.audit_events, tab.audit_chain_length and tab.audit_registry_chains (tablets 0420): the audit viewer serves only the chains the session's role may read, a person filter only on the session's own person unless the role is a governance one; the application role has no SELECT on the audit tables
- Guard: tablets 0430: without a session the application role executes no evidence package, chain extract, scoping report, person activity or chain list, selects no anchor, checkpoint, verification run or retention hold, and sees only RECORD-class rows of the signature seals, ID register, content changes, invalidations and signing failures (row-level security; correction: the rows only, not the planner's count of the others, and since tablets 0610 it selects none of those tables)
- Guard: tab.withheld_from, tab.chain_listed, tab.audit_chains and tab.audit_events (tablets 0440, answers corrected by 0530): whatever their role, the person a data-integrity investigation is about neither lists nor reads its chains, the hold chains it creates, the hold entries on the held records' chains, or the account and person chains of the persons who opened and lead it, nor in those persons' activity anything the investigation wrote; since tablets 0530 each reads as what is not there (no entries, no length, nothing to verify), never as a refusal (0440's EBMR-TAB-020 told the person)
- Guard: tablets 0450: without a session the application role selects no disposal approval, tombstone, restore or restore approval and runs neither integrity list of signatures, so it finds no id of a GOVERNANCE-class signature to ask esig.signature_state about
- Guard: tablets 0460: without a session the application role asks the kernel nothing about what blocks a record (records.open_blockers, has_disposition_blockers, disposal_refusals, retention_of, lock_blocker_epoch) and selects neither the blocker counters nor the retention status view, so it cannot list the persons under investigation and their concern from what blocks a record (the chain verification and the anchor comparison still told which accounts an investigation holds until tablets 0530)
- Guard: tablets 0470: without a session the application role runs none of the kernel's readers of who acted on a chain or a row (iam.chain_persons, iam.record_entry_persons), of the checkpoint of a chain (kernel.checkpoint_chain, which refused only a chain with no entries) or of the entries of a time fault, and selects no segregation evaluation; tab.audit_registry_chains names to the person an investigation is about neither the account and terminal of an entry withheld from them nor a chain withheld from them
- Guard: tablets 0530: without a session the application role verifies no chain (kernel.verify_chain from a position it chose named a chain's length, records.verify_chain) and compares no external anchor with the live chains (continuity.compare_external_anchors, verify_external_anchors, external_anchor_problems), so it learns from neither whether a chain exists; the audit viewer verifies through the session-checked tab.audit_verify_chain (EBMR-TAB-018, EBMR-TAB-020), with the registry chains its view draws on
- Guard: tab.record_withheld, tab.content_for_signing, tab.record_signatures, tab.chain_read_problem, tab.audit_events, tab.audit_chain_length, tab.audit_registry_chains and tab.audit_verify_chain (tablets 0530): the person a data-integrity investigation is about is answered about what is withheld from them as a colleague in the same role is about what is not there (same status, message and body shape): no entries, no length, nothing to verify, no registry chains, no signatures, the content of a record that does not exist; a person filter on its opener or lead is served from the chains the person may read. Known limits (defaults F35.3, F35.4): the account and person chains of the opener and lead, which a colleague lists, reads and verifies, read to the subject as absent (verification 404, not listed), and the hashed sequence numbers and event ids shown skip an entry withheld from the subject
- Guard: tablets 0540: without a session the application role reads no high-water mark or move of a protected sequence (continuity.sequence_high_water, continuity.sequence_bump), so the investigation and hold numbers tell it neither when nor how many investigations and holds were opened (correction: the table statistics still told it until tablets 0570, and the stored authority evaluations until tablets 0580)
- Guard: tab.partly_withheld_chains, tab.audit_chains, tab.audit_chain_length and tab.audit_headless_chains (tablets 0560): on a chain the person a data-integrity investigation is about reads that carries an entry withheld from them (their own account's chain carries the hold of each investigation about them), the chain list, the chain's length and the count its verification reports count, date and order only the entries shown to them, and a chain on which none is shown reads as one that is not there (until 0560 the hold was counted and dated there)
- Guard: tablets 0570: in each installation's database no role that is not a superuser (PUBLIC, so ebmr_app and ebmr_sandbox) selects the per-table, per-index, per-sequence and per-function statistics views or executes their counter functions and the relation size functions, which counted the rows inserted and live in the investigation and hold tables; the migration fails if any stays open. What the application roles still read (pg_stat_activity, pg_locks, pg_stat_ssl, the planner estimates in pg_class, the database-wide counters) is default HW.1 (correction: not the only door left; the stored authority evaluations also told when and how many, and who acted, until tablets 0580, and the planner's view of the signature tables until tablets 0610, which also takes the estimates in pg_class)
- Guard: tablets 0580: without a session the application role (ebmr_app, and ebmr_sandbox through the mirror) selects no row or column of iam.authority_evaluation, whose checks of VALIDATION.MANAGE, DI_INVESTIGATION.APPROVE_CLOSURE and RECORD.LEGAL_HOLD (used only by the investigation and legal-hold commands) told when and how many investigations were opened, linked, refreshed and closed and holds placed and released, and which lead or QA reviewer acted; the migration fails if the role or PUBLIC can still select a column; every reader is SECURITY DEFINER and the application role still evaluates (iam.evaluate_authority). What still changes with those commands for the application role (the authentication of a GOVERNANCE-class signing, the sessions' last activity, the installation-wide audit horizon) is default HW.1 (correction: not all that told it; until tablets 0610 the planner's view of the signature tables (EXPLAIN ANALYZE's count of the rows row-level security removed, narrowed to investigations by a range on the indexed aggregate reference, the row estimates, the row positions and id gaps of the visible rows, the estimates in pg_class) told when and how many investigations' closures were requested and signed, and so which kind of GOVERNANCE-class signing an authentication was for)
- Guard: tablets 0610: the application role (ebmr_app, and ebmr_sandbox through the mirror) selects no row or column of esig.challenge, signature, signature_seal, signature_id_register, content_change, signature_invalidation or signing_failure, so no query plan, row estimate, row position, id or column statistic of theirs tells it how many GOVERNANCE-class signing requests and signatures there are or of which records; the server reads its own signing requests through the session-checked tab.session_challenge (the active session bound to the token, a request made in that session; anything else reads as no request) and signatures through the readers of 0410; and no role but the owner and the superusers selects the planner estimates of pg_class (reltuples, relpages, relallvisible), which counted every row of every table, the investigation tables included; the migration fails if the application role or PUBLIC can still select a column of the seven tables (EBMR-TAB-024) or PUBLIC, an application, authentication or listener role an estimate (EBMR-TAB-025). What the application roles still learn of a GOVERNANCE-class signing is default HW.1
- Guard: tablets 0620: the application role (ebmr_app, and ebmr_sandbox through the mirror) no longer executes esig.record_signing_failure, which logged a failure against any signing request whose id it read in iam.auth_event and, given another person's authentication, raised a HIGH alert naming the request's record and meaning in iam.security_alert, which it reads: so it told which record and kind of GOVERNANCE-class signing an authentication was for; the server logs through the session-checked tab.record_signing_failure, only against a request made in the session the token is or was bound to (else EBMR-ESIG-031, nothing logged or alerted); the migration fails if the role or PUBLIC can still execute the kernel function (EBMR-TAB-026)

- Guard: iam.check_static_segregation: no person holds an ADMINISTRATION and a GMP permission through any account or scope; a role never mixes the two (EBMR-IAM-033)
- Guard: nobody administers their own person, account, credentials, badge or grants (EBMR-IAM-003)
- Guard: iam.assert_not_administrator: GMP data write paths refuse administrator roles and break-glass accounts (EBMR-IAM-042)
- Guard: break-glass: requested by its holder, approved by a second person, time-limited, authentication only inside the window, BREAK_GLASS_USED critical alert on every use, never signs, reviewed after the window
- Guard: iam.assert_not_administrator runs before the permission check in every GMP write path of market (rule tables, site values, policies, pins, evaluations, stub master data), records, continuity (restore approval, number ceilings) and the site batch-number register (EBMR-IAM-042); IT functions (restore operation, backup catalogue, hardening attestations, continuity alarms) are ADMINISTRATION or GOVERNANCE permissions without it

- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/identity/Manifestation.tsx](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/identity/Manifestation.tsx)
- Guard: lockout after authn.lockout_threshold consecutive failed login or signing attempts (default 5, allowed 3-5); sessions end; unlock is an attributed administrator act on the audit chain
- Guard: iam.raise_alert with NOTIFY ebmr_security_alert for failed logins and signings, use of locked or disabled accounts, inactive badges, unknown identifiers and devices; acknowledgement tracked; iam.escalate_overdue_alerts
- Guard: badges: secure technologies only; activation after a passing test at issue; periodic tests (iam.badges_due_for_test); lost or revoked badges de-authorised at once; replacement only after identity re-verification
- Guard: secrets: argon2id verifiers in iam.credential_secret, unreadable by ebmr_app and never in the audit trail; plaintext only in the authentication service (role ebmr_authn)
- Guard: password aging or compromised-password screening must be active (EBMR-IAM-111); policy and history checks when a secret changes
- Guard: identity pinProblems: a new PIN longer than MAX_PIN_LENGTH (12) is refused before hashing (EBMR-IAM-055); generateSecret refuses a policy asking for a longer one (EBMR-IAM-110)
- Guard: tab.record_signing_failure (tablets 0620): every failure the ceremony logs (inside the attempt's command or after its rollback, also after a sign-out or the lock that ended the session) is logged through the kernel's esig.record_signing_failure, with its misuse check and alert, for a request made in the session the server's token is or was bound to; the application role no longer calls the kernel function itself, so it cannot log a failure, or have an alert raised, against a request it did not make (EBMR-ESIG-031)
- Tests: [apps/tablets-server/test/access/attribution.test.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/test/access/attribution.test.ts), ## DI-24

**Corrections, voids and deletions: the original is always preserved.** [DI-24.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/DI-24.yaml). Target phase 1; editions tablets, injectables; IMPLEMENTED.

- Modules: [kernel-audit](../code-map.md#kernel-audit), [kernel-qp-evidence](../code-map.md#kernel-qp-evidence), [kernel-records](../code-map.md#kernel-records), [kernel-platform](../code-map.md#kernel-platform), [kernel-installation](../code-map.md#kernel-installation), [ss-quality](../code-map.md#ss-quality), [rc-lifecycle](../code-map.md#rc-lifecycle), [rc-review](../code-map.md#rc-review), [rc-execution](../code-map.md#rc-execution), [ts-quality](../code-map.md#ts-quality), [ts-review](../code-map.md#ts-review), [ts-execution](../code-map.md#ts-execution), [api-contract](../code-map.md#api-contract), [tools-e2e](../code-map.md#tools-e2e)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0010_audit_trail.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0010_audit_trail.sql)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/corrections.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/corrections.ts) (correction paths a, b, c and dependents)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/data.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/data.ts) (data-point versions)
- Guard: CORRECT_ENTRY (path a): a new data-point version; the original stays with corrector, reason and CORRECTED signature; device-captured values cannot be corrected (ENG-CORRECT-DEVICE); critical data needs approval (S25, ENG-CORRECT-APPROVAL)
- Guard: VOID_ATTEMPT (path b): only an attempt without a result, with a reason and QA approval (S28; ENG-VOID-RESULT, ENG-VOID-APPROVAL); the voided attempt stays in the record
- Guard: Dependents of a correction: derived values recalculated with the old record kept, signed steps sent back for re-verification, stage sign-off invalidated

## DI-28

**Contemporaneous capture, immediate durable commit, late entries and offline buffering.** [DI-28.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/DI-28.yaml). Target phase 4; editions tablets, injectables; VERIFIED.

- Modules: [rc-plant](../code-map.md#rc-plant), [rc-execution](../code-map.md#rc-execution), [ts-access](../code-map.md#ts-access), [ts-devices](../code-map.md#ts-devices), [ts-blocks](../code-map.md#ts-blocks), [ts-review](../code-map.md#ts-review), [ts-execution](../code-map.md#ts-execution), [site-gateway](../code-map.md#site-gateway), [ui-kit](../code-map.md#ui-kit)

- Guard: Late entry: an occurred-at before now needs a reason, must lie between step start and now (ENG-OCCURRED-AT), keeps both times and raises LATE_ENTRY
- Guard: A person-claimed or paper start time is refused (ENG-BACKDATING); only device-synchronised event times drive timers

## DI-29

**Complete data: every attempt, repeat, aborted operation and invalidated result is retained; invalidation needs an investigation.** [DI-29.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/DI-29.yaml). Target phase 4; editions tablets, injectables; VERIFIED.

- Modules: [rc-blocks](../code-map.md#rc-blocks), [rc-execution](../code-map.md#rc-execution), [ts-blocks](../code-map.md#ts-blocks), [ts-execution](../code-map.md#ts-execution), [tools-e2e](../code-map.md#tools-e2e)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/data.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/data.ts) (attempts, repeats, readings)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/corrections.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/corrections.ts) (void, invalidation)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/blocks/generic/recording.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/blocks/generic/recording.ts) (G-13)
- Guard: Every attempt and reading is stored and never replaced; repeats are flagged REPEAT or REPEAT_AFTER_FAIL; the first valid attempt governs until invalidated
- Guard: INVALIDATE_RESULT (path c) needs an investigation reference and QA approval (ENG-INVALIDATE-INVESTIGATION); the original stays
- Guard: A readings set must hold the planned number of readings (ENG-VALUE-READINGS)

- Guard: Sandboxed language with a closed function library (library/index.ts): no assignment, statements, loops, lambdas, recursion or user-defined functions; reserved words refused (EXPR-SYN-006, EXPR-SYN-020); unknown functions refused (EXPR-FN-001); static cost bound (EXPR-COST-001)
- Guard: Checker: reads declared inputs only (EXPR-TYP-001); dimension and unit checks (EXPR-UNIT-010/011/012/013/014); semantic tags, e.g. % LOD + % yield refused (EXPR-TAG-001/002/003); a constant typed as a calculation refused (EXPR-INV25-001)
- Guard: Exact arithmetic: Dec at 34 significant digits on a private decimal.js clone, strings only (JavaScript numbers refused); unit factors are exact rationals generated from UCUM definitions and cross-checked with @lhncbc/ucum-lhc (regenerate-and-compare test)
- Guard: Explicit evaluation errors naming the input path: MISSING_INPUT, DIVISION_BY_ZERO, DOMAIN_ERROR, EMPTY_SERIES, SERIES_TOO_LONG, INVALID_INPUT; never a silent zero or NaN
- Guard: Evaluation record (evidence/record.ts): expression and canonical text, language version, program hash, inputs with value, unit, tag and source reference, every conversion with its exact factor, table lookups, full-precision output, SHA-256 content hash
- Guard: recalculate(): a correction produces a new record that supersedes the earlier one by content hash, with the reason and each corrected input; the earlier record is never changed (both kept)
- Guard: verifyEvaluationRecord() and DERIVED inputs (INV-25): a stored result must recompute identically from its recorded inputs; a typed or altered result, or a result of another calculation, is refused
- Guard: Rounding only at comparison and display: compareToLimit / compareToBound with USP_HALF_UP_AT_LIMIT_DECIMALS or PH_EUR_SIG_FIGS, ALL_APPLY per criterion; full precision always kept
- Guard: Legacy hardness unit: 'kg/cm² (as kgf/kp)' maps to kgf with 1 kgf = 9.80665 N and a WARNING needing justification (EXPR-UNIT-030); a pressure unit on a force parameter refused (EXPR-UNIT-021)
- Guard: Dependency graph: calculation cycles refused (EXPR-DEP-001); deterministic evaluation order; dependents of a corrected input classified as derived result, decision input or executed target (outline 5.2.3)
- Guard: Derived values are computed by the engine through @ebmr/expr with an evaluation record; typing one is refused (ENG-DERIVED-TYPED); block-computed quantities (net = gross - tare, container totals) cannot be typed (ENG-NOT-RECORDED-HERE)
- Guard: A calculation that cannot be evaluated raises CALCULATION_FAILED, never a silent zero
- Guard: After a correction the dependents are recalculated: the old record is kept and superseded with the reason; signed steps go back for re-verification; an executed target outside tolerance raises EXECUTED_TARGET_CHANGED
- Guard: G-20 yield needs a verifier (G20-NO-VERIFIER)

## DI-34

**Operational sequencing checks.** [DI-34.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/DI-34.yaml). Target phase 2; editions tablets, injectables; VERIFIED.

- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0200_execution_store.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0200_execution_store.sql), 0210_execution_writers.sql (one row per engine call, refusals included; hash chain; projection; execution_violations())
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/execution](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/execution) (ExecutionTx, issueBatch, replayExecution)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/replay.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/replay.ts), codec.ts, actions.ts (replay at stored clocks; availableActions by dry run)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/execution](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/execution) (command route: lock, engine roles, intent resolution, engine.step, append; timeline; verify-replay)
- Guard: TokenGame.fire: only a node holding its tokens in the current marking fires, so no step runs before its predecessor (out-of-sequence refused)
- Guard: TokenGame loop test: CONTINUE only below maxIterations; at the maximum an unmet exit is EXHAUSTED (exception)
- Guard: Validator structure gates FLOW-ID-UNIQUE, FLOW-LEVELS, FLOW-CONSTRUCTS, FLOW-LIBRARY, FLOW-LOOPS, FLOW-REPLICATES, FLOW-DECISIONS
- Guard: Graph gates GRAPH-START-END, GRAPH-REACHABLE, GRAPH-TERMINATES, GRAPH-DECLARED-CYCLES, GRAPH-MATCHED, GRAPH-BOUNDARIES
- Guard: Data gates DATA-WRITTEN-BEFORE-READ, DATA-GUARDS (exhaustive, non-overlapping, reachable), DATA-PARALLEL (no unsequenced writes or claims)
- Guard: step(state, command, clock): only a step instance holding a token is enabled and may start, record or sign (ENG-NOT-ENABLED); its token moves on only when COMPLETED or SKIPPED; control nodes fire automatically; untaken branches become NOT_APPLICABLE by the system
- Guard: Data-decided branches and loop exits are decided from recorded data, never by a person; operator choices and run-time sub-lot counts need a role and a reason (ENG-DECISION-MODE, ENG-REPLICATE-COUNT, ENG-LOOP-EXIT)
- Guard: Batch on hold: nothing starts, completes or advances (ENG-BATCH-ON-HOLD); stale commands are refused (ENG-STALE)
- Guard: recipe-core 0326 (F58.1): the database holds the engine's version check: a command from a screen that showed another version of the run is recorded only as the refusal ENG-STALE, that refusal only for such a command, and it changes nothing but the time sweep's findings (EBMR-EXE-140); the scale readings a stale weighing names are named, not used, and checked as the claims of its command would check them together, tare then gross (tablets 0500, 0510); no reading is claimed under the correlation id of a stale refusal, checked at commit whatever the order or transaction, and the readings it names are exactly the stored tare and gross (tablets 0520, EBMR-EXE-124)
- Guard: checkInvariants after every command in the model-based tests: tokens safe and matching the enabled steps; no step started before its predecessors completed; simulator paths and engine completions agree
- Guard: recipe.append_execution: gapless sequence and hash chain (EBMR-EXE-023), state and events hashes recomputed in SQL, unique command id (EBMR-EXE-024); refused engine calls are rows with their refusal
- Guard: replayExecution: the stored rows re-executed with the pinned engine and block versions give the stored accepted flags, events and states row by row; a batch pinned to another engine or registry is refused (EBMR-EXE-061, 062)
- Guard: Screens offer only what the engine accepts (availableActions dry-runs engine.step); the command itself is decided again by the engine in the command transaction

## DI-35

**Electronic-signature catalogue: every predicate-rule signature point.** [DI-35.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/DI-35.yaml). Target phase 1; editions tablets, injectables; SPECIFIED.

- Modules: [kernel-esig](../code-map.md#kernel-esig), [kernel-market](../code-map.md#kernel-market), [kernel-qp-evidence](../code-map.md#kernel-qp-evidence), [kernel-authorization](../code-map.md#kernel-authorization), [kernel-installation](../code-map.md#kernel-installation), [ss-india](../code-map.md#ss-india), [ss-quality](../code-map.md#ss-quality), [ss-lots](../code-map.md#ss-lots), [ss-core](../code-map.md#ss-core), [rc-lifecycle](../code-map.md#rc-lifecycle), [rc-equipment-data](../code-map.md#rc-equipment-data), [rc-library](../code-map.md#rc-library), [rc-formula](../code-map.md#rc-formula), [rc-india](../code-map.md#rc-india), [rc-execution](../code-map.md#rc-execution), [rc-flow](../code-map.md#rc-flow), [ts-access](../code-map.md#ts-access), [ts-esig](../code-map.md#ts-esig), [ts-designer](../code-map.md#ts-designer), [ts-market-config](../code-map.md#ts-market-config), [ts-quality](../code-map.md#ts-quality), [ts-records](../code-map.md#ts-records), [ts-execution](../code-map.md#ts-execution), [ts-administration](../code-map.md#ts-administration), [ts-core](../code-map.md#ts-core), [ui-kit](../code-map.md#ui-kit), [tools-e2e](../code-map.md#tools-e2e)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0210_esig_catalogue.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0210_esig_catalogue.sql) (esig.meaning_type catalogue, esig.predicate_signature_point inventory, quality-unit configuration)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0230_esig_sign.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0230_esig_sign.sql) (esig.create_challenge, esig.sign, esig.check_independence, esig.check_witness)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0250_esig_stub.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0250_esig_stub.sql) (stub signable entries; esig.stub_complete: completion only by a signature)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/esig/catalogue.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/esig/catalogue.ts)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/esig/signing.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/esig/signing.ts)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/guards.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/guards.ts) (signature meanings per engine signature point)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/esig/index.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/esig/index.ts) (content, challenge, sign: authenticate, dry-run, esig.sign and seal, apply in one command; failures logged after rollback)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/esig/badges.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/esig/badges.ts)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/esig/views.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/esig/views.ts) (manifestation from the stored copy)
- Code: [apps/tablets-web/src/features/access/ceremony.tsx](../../../apps/tablets-web/src/features/access/ceremony.tsx) (SignatureCeremonyPort)
- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/machines/ceremony.ts](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/machines/ceremony.ts)
- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/identity/SignatureDialog.tsx](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/identity/SignatureDialog.tsx)
- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/identity/SignableContent.tsx](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/identity/SignableContent.tsx)
- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/identity/Manifestation.tsx](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/identity/Manifestation.tsx)
- Guard: esig.current_meaning: only active catalogue types can be challenged or signed (EBMR-ESIG-026); no acknowledgement-only type exists
- Guard: esig.sign: authority for one of the type's permissions evaluated and stored at the instant of signing (iam.evaluate_authority, INV-26); delegation validity (EBMR-ESIG-041); independence rules evaluated on the person (EBMR-ESIG-042 to 045); WITNESSED rules: same terminal or a terminal of the same room, within the witness window, before the next step (EBMR-ESIG-050 to 053)
- Guard: esig.stub_complete: a signature point is completed only by a valid signature of the required meaning, never by an acknowledgement (EBMR-ESIG-092, 093)
- Guard: esig.add_meaning_type, esig.configure_meaning_type, esig.retire_meaning_type: quality unit only (MBR.APPROVE), never an administrator
- Guard: Every engine signature point requires its kernel meaning (PERFORMED, VERIFIED, WITNESSED, CORRECTED, HOLD_POINT_RELEASED, ...); a signature by someone else, with another meaning or used twice is refused (ENG-SIGNATURE, ENG-SIGNATURE-MEANING, ENG-SIGNATURE-REUSED)
- Guard: A step whose check is a witness (the MBR's WITNESSED, or a critical value typed by hand without the photo or printout its MBR names) asks for WITNESSED only while a witness is possible; once the engine's witness window after the act has passed or the run moved on, its later review is the check and raises WITNESS_LATE, which only QA dispositions, and a late WITNESSED is refused (engine 1.5.0; recipe-core 0333 recipe.step_check_meanings, recipe.late_check_record, trigger zzf_execution_late_check, EBMR-EXE-141; default T12.2). The facts it is decided from are the record's: a step's start and end only by its own act at that row's trusted database time and kept as recorded, a value at its row's trusted time (recipe-core 0334 recipe.step_time_problem, trigger zzg_execution_step_times, EBMR-EXE-142 to 145), and a batch pinned with a witness window no shorter than the site's kernel window and no longer than the kernel allows (recipe.witness_window_problem, trigger ac_execution_witness_window, EBMR-EXE-146; default T12.4)

- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0200_execution_store.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0200_execution_store.sql), 0210_execution_writers.sql (versioned signed content tables registered with esig; content providers; disposition proposals)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/execution/signable.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/execution/signable.ts) (ENGINE_COMMAND: preview, prepare, dry run, apply)
- Code: [packages/api-contract/src/common.ts](../../../packages/api-contract/src/common.ts) (storableText, unstorableTextIssues: text no record can hold), [packages/api-contract/src/route.ts](../../../packages/api-contract/src/route.ts) (defineRoute applies it)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/platform/db/pools.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/platform/db/pools.ts) (refuseUnstorableParameters), [apps/tablets-server/src/platform/http/json-body.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/platform/http/json-body.ts) (strict UTF-8 request bodies), [apps/tablets-server/src/platform/http/errors.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/platform/http/errors.ts) (unstorableTextRefusal)
- Code: [apps/tablets-web/src/features/designer/model/ops.ts](../../../apps/tablets-web/src/features/designer/model/ops.ts) (an edit that would bring text no record can hold into the MBR content is refused, naming the field, so the designer never hashes such content; review F31 round 2)
- Guard: esig.create_challenge: the server rebuilds the canonical content and refuses a displayed hash or version that differs (EBMR-ESIG-012, 013)
- Guard: esig.sign: the record must still have the challenged version and hash (EBMR-ESIG-012); signing and changing a record are serialised by a transaction lock
- Guard: esig.on_signed_content_change (trigger on every registered signed table): a permitted change supersedes every affected signature in the same transaction through an insert-only SUPERSEDED_BY_CHANGE row
- Guard: esig.assert_signature and esig.platform_signature_check: a signature is used only for its own record and meaning and only while valid (EBMR-ESIG-070)
- Guard: esig.assert_change_triggers_ok: no challenge or signature while an invalidation trigger is missing or disabled (EBMR-ESIG-008)
- Guard: The preview is the content prepared at database time (sweep and PERFORMED finalisation); the challenge prepares it for real and the kernel refuses a differing hash (EBMR-ESIG-013)
- Guard: An exception disposition signs its proposed outcome, justification and deviation (proposedDisposition in the batch_exception content); a differing action at signing is refused (EBMR-EXE-086)
- Guard: apply asserts the signed content is unchanged by the command; any failure rolls back signature, seal and row together (EBMR-EXE-082, 083)
- Guard: canonicalJson (kernel esig/canonical.ts, expr evidence/hash.ts): text no record can hold (U+0000, a UTF-16 surrogate without its other half) has no canonical form and is refused (EBMR-ESIG-004; UnstorableTextError), as esig.canonical_json can never receive it, so no client hashes content the server cannot store (review F31)
- Guard: API contract (defineRoute applies storableText): the params, query and body of every route refuse such text at any depth, naming the field (400 INVALID_INPUT); the preview refuses it in the decision it decodes (parseIntent); the server refuses bodies that are not UTF-8 and any query parameter holding such text; jsonb and text refuse it in the database (22P05, 22P02, 22021), answered 400, never 500 (review F30, F32, F33)

- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0230_esig_sign.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0230_esig_sign.sql) (esig.required_components, esig.component_problem, esig.continuous_session_start, esig.sign)

- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/machines/ceremony.ts](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/machines/ceremony.ts)
- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/identity/SignatureDialog.tsx](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/identity/SignatureDialog.tsx)

- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/platform/auth/attribution.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/platform/auth/attribution.ts) (per-entry attribution: proof over the exact request, BADGE_TAP or TAP_PIN, same person or 409 SWITCH_USER, tap rate limit)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/access/index.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/access/index.ts) (lock, unlock by the same person on the same terminal, keep-alive with a tap, switch user by badge and PIN)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0100_tab_attribution.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0100_tab_attribution.sql) (attribution method per shop-floor class, audited history)
- Code: [apps/tablets-web/src/features/access/controller.tsx](../../../apps/tablets-web/src/features/access/controller.tsx) (BadgeTapPort, switch offers, lock overlay, session poll)
- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/machines/kiosk-lock.ts](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/machines/kiosk-lock.ts)
- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/identity/LockOverlay.tsx](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/identity/LockOverlay.tsx)
- Code: [packages/ui-kit/src/identity/BadgePrompt.tsx](../../../packages/ui-kit/src/identity/BadgePrompt.tsx)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/platform/http/bind.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/platform/http/bind.ts) (admitSession: the session gate of every request, asked again by open streams)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/devices/routes.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/devices/routes.ts) (live display stream: ends when its session no longer serves it)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0550_session_terminal_key.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0550_session_terminal_key.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0610_signature_table_plan_reads.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0610_signature_table_plan_reads.sql) (tab.session_challenge)
- Code: [apps/tablets-web/src/api/page-binding.ts](../../../apps/tablets-web/src/api/page-binding.ts)
- Guard: esig.component_problem: two distinct components (user ID + password or badge + PIN); biometric or unknown components refused; MFA component when esig.mfa_at_signing is on (EBMR-ESIG-038)
- Guard: esig.required_components: all components at every signing by default; the QA relaxation lets only the session owner, for routine types, after a first full signing in the same continuous session, use the private component; never for master-record, release or certification types; a co-signature always needs all components
- Guard: esig.continuous_session_start: the continuous session restarts at every lock or unlock (an inactivity timeout ends in an unlock); a terminal change is a new session
- Guard: iam.consume_auth_event (called by esig.sign): the event is the signer's own, bound to this challenge, fresh and unused; the performer's authentication never supplies another person's signature (EBMR-IAM-093)
- Guard: esig.set_policy: policy values changed only by the quality unit with a reason and a change-control reference, within their allowed ranges
- Guard: devices.live (SSE-SESSION): an open live display asks the session gate again every 2 s (tab.resolve_session through admitSession) and ends, naming the refusal, once the session is locked (by the person or the idle time), signed out, switched, expired or works in a role no longer granted (which ends the session too); the database decides the session's state
- Guard: pageBindingRefusal (bind.ts) on tab.resolve_session's terminal key (tablets 0550): a request whose page names its terminal key or the person its session started with is served as nobody when the cookie's session was opened on another terminal (401 SESSION_OTHER_TERMINAL) or is another person's (401 SESSION_CHANGED); the SPA names both on every request (page-binding.ts, the person kept per tab) and then shows the sign-in form or says that its sign-in changed, never working in that session (review F41, round 4; residuals in decision F41.1)
- Guard: tab.session_challenge (tablets 0610, HIGH-WATER round 5): the ceremony reads back the signing request it signs, the signature made for it and its correlation only for the active session bound to the token the server presents, and only a request made in that session; another session's request, an unknown token or a locked session reads as no request (the sign route then refuses EBMR-ESIG-031); the application role selects no row of esig.challenge or esig.signature itself
- Tests: [apps/tablets-server/test/access/session.test.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/test/access/session.test.ts), ## DI-50

**Data-integrity investigations, remediation and scoping support.** [DI-50.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/DI-50.yaml). Target phase 1; editions tablets, injectables; SPECIFIED.

- Modules: [kernel-records](../code-map.md#kernel-records), [kernel-authorization](../code-map.md#kernel-authorization), [kernel-installation](../code-map.md#kernel-installation), [ts-access](../code-map.md#ts-access), [ts-audit](../code-map.md#ts-audit), [ts-esig](../code-map.md#ts-esig), [ts-operations](../code-map.md#ts-operations), [ts-validation](../code-map.md#ts-validation)
- Code: [packages/kernel/src/records/investigations.ts](../../../packages/kernel/src/records/investigations.ts)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0430_records_di_investigations.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0430_records_di_investigations.sql)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0440_records_blockers_and_purge.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0440_records_blockers_and_purge.sql)
- Code: [packages/kernel/sql/0720_authorization_signatures.sql](../../../packages/kernel/sql/0720_authorization_signatures.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0440_investigation_subject_reads.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0440_investigation_subject_reads.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0460_disposition_blocker_reads.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0460_disposition_blocker_reads.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0470_audit_actor_readers.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0470_audit_actor_readers.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0530_chain_verification_and_subject_answers.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0530_chain_verification_and_subject_answers.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0540_sequence_high_water_reads.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0540_sequence_high_water_reads.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0560_subject_counts_shown_entries.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0560_subject_counts_shown_entries.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0570_table_statistics_reads.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0570_table_statistics_reads.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0580_authority_evaluation_reads.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0580_authority_evaluation_reads.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0610_signature_table_plan_reads.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0610_signature_table_plan_reads.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/0620_signing_failure_log_session.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/0620_signing_failure_log_session.sql)
- Guard: records.investigation_scope / records.person_activity: every record the person created or changed in the window, from the audit trail
- Guard: records.open_di_investigation: links and holds every record in scope; person investigated cannot open or lead it
- Guard: records.open_blockers: linked records, and records the person touched in the window, block disposition until closure
- Guard: records.close_di_investigation: conclusion, remediation and an approver who is neither investigator nor subject; releases the holds
- Guard: records.close_di_investigation accepts only the approver's own valid DI_INVESTIGATION_CLOSED signature of the investigation (di_investigation:&lt;id&gt;; EBMR-REC-065)
- Guard: tablets 0410 and 0420: a data-integrity investigation's content, signatures and audit chains are never served to the person it is about (since tablets 0530 answered as what is not there); the application role has no SELECT on the investigation tables
- Guard: tablets 0430: without a session the application role executes no evidence package, chain extract, scoping report, person activity or chain list, selects no anchor, checkpoint, verification run or retention hold, and sees only RECORD-class rows of the signature seals, ID register, content changes, invalidations and signing failures (row-level security; correction: the rows only, not the planner's count of the others, and since tablets 0610 it selects none of those tables)
- Guard: tab.withheld_from, tab.chain_listed, tab.audit_chains and tab.audit_events (tablets 0440, answers corrected by 0530): whatever their role, the person a data-integrity investigation is about neither lists nor reads its chains, the hold chains it creates, the hold entries on the held records' chains, or the account and person chains of the persons who opened and lead it, nor in those persons' activity anything the investigation wrote; since tablets 0530 each reads as what is not there (no entries, no length, nothing to verify), never as a refusal (0440's EBMR-TAB-020 told the person)
- Guard: tablets 0450: without a session the application role selects no disposal approval, tombstone, restore or restore approval and runs neither integrity list of signatures, so it finds no id of a GOVERNANCE-class signature to ask esig.signature_state about
- Guard: tablets 0460: without a session the application role asks the kernel nothing about what blocks a record (records.open_blockers, has_disposition_blockers, disposal_refusals, retention_of, lock_blocker_epoch) and selects neither the blocker counters nor the retention status view, so it cannot list the persons under investigation and their concern from what blocks a record (the chain verification and the anchor comparison still told which accounts an investigation holds until tablets 0530)
- Guard: tablets 0470: without a session the application role runs none of the kernel's readers of who acted on a chain or a row (iam.chain_persons, iam.record_entry_persons), of the checkpoint of a chain (kernel.checkpoint_chain, which refused only a chain with no entries) or of the entries of a time fault, and selects no segregation evaluation; tab.audit_registry_chains names to the person an investigation is about neither the account and terminal of an entry withheld from them nor a chain withheld from them
- Guard: tablets 0530: without a session the application role verifies no chain (kernel.verify_chain from a position it chose named a chain's length, records.verify_chain) and compares no external anchor with the live chains (continuity.compare_external_anchors, verify_external_anchors, external_anchor_problems), so it learns from neither whether a chain exists; the audit viewer verifies through the session-checked tab.audit_verify_chain (EBMR-TAB-018, EBMR-TAB-020), with the registry chains its view draws on
- Guard: tab.record_withheld, tab.content_for_signing, tab.record_signatures, tab.chain_read_problem, tab.audit_events, tab.audit_chain_length, tab.audit_registry_chains and tab.audit_verify_chain (tablets 0530): the person a data-integrity investigation is about is answered about what is withheld from them as a colleague in the same role is about what is not there (same status, message and body shape): no entries, no length, nothing to verify, no registry chains, no signatures, the content of a record that does not exist; a person filter on its opener or lead is served from the chains the person may read. Known limits (defaults F35.3, F35.4): the account and person chains of the opener and lead, which a colleague lists, reads and verifies, read to the subject as absent (verification 404, not listed), and the hashed sequence numbers and event ids shown skip an entry withheld from the subject
- Guard: tablets 0540: without a session the application role reads no high-water mark or move of a protected sequence (continuity.sequence_high_water, continuity.sequence_bump), so the investigation and hold numbers tell it neither when nor how many investigations and holds were opened (correction: the table statistics still told it until tablets 0570, and the stored authority evaluations until tablets 0580)
- Guard: tab.partly_withheld_chains, tab.audit_chains, tab.audit_chain_length and tab.audit_headless_chains (tablets 0560): on a chain the person a data-integrity investigation is about reads that carries an entry withheld from them (their own account's chain carries the hold of each investigation about them), the chain list, the chain's length and the count its verification reports count, date and order only the entries shown to them, and a chain on which none is shown reads as one that is not there (until 0560 the hold was counted and dated there)
- Guard: tablets 0570: in each installation's database no role that is not a superuser (PUBLIC, so ebmr_app and ebmr_sandbox) selects the per-table, per-index, per-sequence and per-function statistics views or executes their counter functions and the relation size functions, which counted the rows inserted and live in the investigation and hold tables; the migration fails if any stays open. What the application roles still read (pg_stat_activity, pg_locks, pg_stat_ssl, the planner estimates in pg_class, the database-wide counters) is default HW.1 (correction: not the only door left; the stored authority evaluations also told when and how many, and who acted, until tablets 0580, and the planner's view of the signature tables until tablets 0610, which also takes the estimates in pg_class)
- Guard: tablets 0580: without a session the application role (ebmr_app, and ebmr_sandbox through the mirror) selects no row or column of iam.authority_evaluation, whose checks of VALIDATION.MANAGE, DI_INVESTIGATION.APPROVE_CLOSURE and RECORD.LEGAL_HOLD (used only by the investigation and legal-hold commands) told when and how many investigations were opened, linked, refreshed and closed and holds placed and released, and which lead or QA reviewer acted; the migration fails if the role or PUBLIC can still select a column; every reader is SECURITY DEFINER and the application role still evaluates (iam.evaluate_authority). What still changes with those commands for the application role (the authentication of a GOVERNANCE-class signing, the sessions' last activity, the installation-wide audit horizon) is default HW.1 (correction: not all that told it; until tablets 0610 the planner's view of the signature tables (EXPLAIN ANALYZE's count of the rows row-level security removed, narrowed to investigations by a range on the indexed aggregate reference, the row estimates, the row positions and id gaps of the visible rows, the estimates in pg_class) told when and how many investigations' closures were requested and signed, and so which kind of GOVERNANCE-class signing an authentication was for)
- Guard: tablets 0610: the application role (ebmr_app, and ebmr_sandbox through the mirror) selects no row or column of esig.challenge, signature, signature_seal, signature_id_register, content_change, signature_invalidation or signing_failure, so no query plan, row estimate, row position, id or column statistic of theirs tells it how many GOVERNANCE-class signing requests and signatures there are or of which records; the server reads its own signing requests through the session-checked tab.session_challenge (the active session bound to the token, a request made in that session; anything else reads as no request) and signatures through the readers of 0410; and no role but the owner and the superusers selects the planner estimates of pg_class (reltuples, relpages, relallvisible), which counted every row of every table, the investigation tables included; the migration fails if the application role or PUBLIC can still select a column of the seven tables (EBMR-TAB-024) or PUBLIC, an application, authentication or listener role an estimate (EBMR-TAB-025). What the application roles still learn of a GOVERNANCE-class signing is default HW.1
- Guard: tablets 0620: the application role (ebmr_app, and ebmr_sandbox through the mirror) no longer executes esig.record_signing_failure, which logged a failure against any signing request whose id it read in iam.auth_event and, given another person's authentication, raised a HIGH alert naming the request's record and meaning in iam.security_alert, which it reads: so it told which record and kind of GOVERNANCE-class signing an authentication was for; the server logs through the session-checked tab.record_signing_failure, only against a request made in the session the token is or was bound to (else EBMR-ESIG-031, nothing logged or alerted); the migration fails if the role or PUBLIC can still execute the kernel function (EBMR-TAB-026)
- Tests: [apps/tablets-server/test/audit/read-authorisation.test.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/test/audit/read-authorisation.test.ts), [apps/tablets-server/test/esig/read-authorisation.test.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/test/esig/read-authorisation.test.ts), [apps/tablets-server/test/mutation/read-authorisation.test.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/test/mutation/read-authorisation.test.ts), [apps/tablets-server/test/operations/session-checked-reads.test.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/test/operations/session-checked-reads.test.ts), [apps/tablets-server/test/validation/records-register-routes.test.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/test/validation/records-register-routes.test.ts), [packages/kernel/test/records/investigations.test.ts](../../../packages/kernel/test/records/investigations.test.ts), [packages/kernel/test/records/retention-purge.test.ts](../../../packages/kernel/test/records/retention-purge.test.ts), [packages/kernel/test/review/f30.test.ts](../../../packages/kernel/test/review/f30.test.ts), [packages/kernel/test/review/f35.test.ts](../../../packages/kernel/test/review/f35.test.ts)

- Guard: mbr_version.validator_passed (DRAFT -&gt; IN_VERIFICATION): the automated consistency checks of the static validator (units, formulae, limits, BOM, data entry) pass with zero errors and justified warnings on the exact content, before any check signature is possible
- Guard: mbr_version.prepared_by_author and mbr_version.signed_in_state: PREPARED by the version author, every signature applied at its own step

- Guard: mbr_version.effective_date_set: no approval without an effective date; a market authorisation never takes effect before the version effective date (recipe.activate_market_authorisation)

- Guard: mbr_version.regulatory_confirmed (CHECKED -&gt; APPROVED): approval requires a valid regulatory-conformity sign-off (REGULATORY_CONFIRMED, permission MBR.REGULATORY_CONFIRM) of the version for the markets of its content
- Guard: mbr_version.registered_ranges_kept (CHECKED -&gt; APPROVED): a changed registered element (REGISTERED-tier range or REGISTRATION reference) needs a regulatory assessment for every market of the version; a widened registered range also the variation, supplement or approval reference
- Guard: recipe.propose_market_authorisation refuses a market the master content does not list (EBMR-RCP-081)
- Guard: recipe.select_version_for_issue refuses a market set with a market no version is CURRENT or BRIDGING for, naming each market (the master is not linked to it)
