## PR-05

**Master versioning, supersession, periodic review, retirement and document inventory.** [PR-05.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/PR-05.yaml). Target phase 2; editions tablets, injectables; SPECIFIED.

- Modules: [ss-reporting](../code-map.md#ss-reporting), [rc-lifecycle](../code-map.md#rc-lifecycle), [rc-library](../code-map.md#rc-library), [ts-designer](../code-map.md#ts-designer), [ts-market-config](../code-map.md#ts-market-config), [ts-dashboards](../code-map.md#ts-dashboards), [ts-core](../code-map.md#ts-core), [tools-e2e](../code-map.md#tools-e2e)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0160_market_authorisation.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0160_market_authorisation.sql) (supersession per market and scope in the same transaction, withdrawal, recipe.retire_version)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0170_batch_issue.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0170_batch_issue.sql) (selection at issue, pin record, in-flight dispositions)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0180_review_inventory_invariants.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0180_review_inventory_invariants.sql) (periodic review, overdue list, document inventory, where-used)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/lifecycle/inventory.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/lifecycle/inventory.ts) (CSV export, reconciliation with the site document list)
- Guard: batch_mbr_pin trigger: a new batch pins only the version the selection gives at the issue instant; after v2 is CURRENT, v1 is refused (EBMR-RCP-101)
- Guard: mbr_version.review_due_set: every approved master carries a review due date after its effective date; recipe.overdue_reviews lists the overdue ones (the dashboard that shows them comes with Phase 6: open, see phase-2-open-questions F13.2)
- Guard: mbr_version.no_open_authorisation and recipe.retire_version: retirement ends every open authorisation; retired versions are never selected, issued records stay readable (recipe.verify_control_recipe)
- Guard: mbr_version.in_flight_dispositioned: issued and running batches of earlier versions get CONTINUE (default), HOLD_FOR_ASSESSMENT or CANCEL_UNSTARTED before a successor is approved

- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0110_mbr_family_version.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0110_mbr_family_version.sql) (content revisions accepted only in DRAFT; version identity immutable)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/sql/0130_mbr_guards.sql](../../../packages/recipe-core/sql/0130_mbr_guards.sql) (recipe.process_impact, guards change_record_linked and change_control_complete)

- Code: [apps/tablets-web/src/features/designer](../../../apps/tablets-web/src/features/designer) (read-only unless DRAFT; stale-revision refusal shown; unsaved edits kept as a local draft only, decision D7)

- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/simulator/boundary.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/simulator/boundary.ts) (limit boundary vectors)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/data.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/data.ts) (limit evaluation at recording)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/exceptions.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/exceptions.ts) (tiers and blocking)
- Code: [apps/tablets-web/src/features/designer/components/DocumentView.tsx](../../../apps/tablets-web/src/features/designer/components/DocumentView.tsx) (limit tiers, targets and time bounds edited as decimal text through DecimalField)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/designer/validation.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/designer/validation.ts) (GMP-LIMITS findings located at the parameter)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/src/features/execution/views.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/src/features/execution/views.ts) (limits with tier and source reference, the stored verdict of each attempt, blocking exceptions with what to do)
- Guard: checkLimitSpec / checkNestingAfterRounding: alert inside internal action inside registered and validated, exactly and after each tier rounds
- Guard: evaluateLimits: value kept as acquired; outermost breached tier decides: REGISTER_ITEM, STOP_UNTIL_DEVIATION, REGISTRATION_RELEVANT_DEVIATION (QA-only, sign-off blocked). The release screen that shows the flag comes with Phase 4 (open; see phase-2-open-questions F13.1)
- Guard: evaluateAllApply: every criterion with its own rounding and sampling; sampling plan = union
- Guard: At recording the value is stored as acquired and evaluated: ALERT a register item; INTERNAL_ACTION stops the step and the steps after it until a deviation is linked; REGISTERED or VALIDATED a registration-relevant deviation that only QA dispositions and that blocks the stage sign-off; the operator cannot clear it
- Guard: Loop endpoint limits are judged at loop exit; every iteration is kept
- Guard: Sandbox test runs: an internal-action breach is recorded as acquired, the exception blocks, and signing the step is refused (BLOCKED_BY_EXCEPTION) until a supervisor links a deviation
- Tests: [apps/tablets-server/test/blocks/blister/blocks.test.ts](../../../apps/tablets-server/test/blocks/blister/blocks.test.ts), ## PR-13

**Step-sequence enforcement and the in-line deviation path.** [PR-13.yaml](../../../compliance/controls/PR-13.yaml). Target phase 4; editions tablets, injectables; VERIFIED.

- Modules: [rc-lifecycle](../code-map.md#rc-lifecycle), [rc-blocks](../code-map.md#rc-blocks), [rc-execution](../code-map.md#rc-execution), [ts-blocks](../code-map.md#ts-blocks), [ts-execution](../code-map.md#ts-execution), [tools-e2e](../code-map.md#tools-e2e)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/steps.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/steps.ts) (requestSkip, chooseAlternative)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/batch.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/batch.ts) (raiseException, attachDeviation, dispositionException)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/guards.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/guards.ts)
- Guard: Start, record or sign out of sequence is refused (ENG-NOT-ENABLED)
- Guard: REQUEST_SKIP only for steps the MBR declares skippable, approved by a different person, never for CPP/CQA inputs, yield, clearance, dispensing, charge-in, sign-off or hold points (ENG-SKIP-FORBIDDEN); it raises STEP_SKIPPED
- Guard: Exceptions: an ACTION exception blocks until a deviation is linked (ENG-DEVIATION-REQUIRED); it is dispositioned by a person outside the involvement set of its step (ENG-SEGREGATION)

- Modules: [ss-quality](../code-map.md#ss-quality), [rc-blocks](../code-map.md#rc-blocks), [rc-library](../code-map.md#rc-library), [rc-execution](../code-map.md#rc-execution), [rc-flow](../code-map.md#rc-flow), [ts-designer](../code-map.md#ts-designer), [ts-devices](../code-map.md#ts-devices), [ts-blocks](../code-map.md#ts-blocks), [ts-quality](../code-map.md#ts-quality), [ts-execution](../code-map.md#ts-execution), [ts-core](../code-map.md#ts-core), [api-contract](../code-map.md#api-contract), [tools-e2e](../code-map.md#tools-e2e)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/blocks/generic/material.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/blocks/generic/material.ts) (G-07)
- Code: [packages/recipe-core/src/engine/corrections.ts](../../../packages/recipe-core/src/engine/corrections.ts) (executed targets)
- Code: [packages/site-services/sql/0740_lims_lite.sql](../../../packages/site-services/sql/0740_lims_lite.sql)
- Code: [apps/tablets-server/sql/1001_lot_assay_from_lims.sql](../../../apps/tablets-server/sql/1001_lot_assay_from_lims.sql)
- Guard: G-07: net = gross - tare computed; the dispensed total is compared with its target within the MBR tolerance (DISPENSE_OUT_OF_TOLERANCE); every BOM line is dispensed before PERFORMED (G07-BOM-INCOMPLETE); a target without tolerance is a warning that needs a justification (G07-TOLERANCE-MISSING)
- Guard: A corrected input that moves an executed target outside tolerance raises EXECUTED_TARGET_CHANGED; the dispensed quantity is never recomputed
- Guard: LIMS-lite: verdicts computed by the database from decimal limits; approval by a QC person other than the recorder (QC_RESULT_APPROVED); a revision drops the approval; on GMP the lot assay is copied from approved results named by id, never typed, and is read only while they stay approved (EBMR-SITE-200 to 205, EBMR-EXE-606)

- Guard: recipe-core 0345: in a test run an accepted START_STEP records no start data (the quality unit's authorisation of a reprocessing is not given through a test run), and start data names its authoriser by the id of a person of this installation (EBMR-EXE-126)
- Guard: recipe-core 0621/0622, tablets 1021: an addition (G-24@2, TB-08) stands on source facts read by the database, per-use QA authorisation (REPROCESSING_AUTHORISED, never the adder's), same product and strength, case-matching status (reserved residue or QA-released), MBR age, percentage and source-batch limits, chained recovery blocked; refused rows EBMR-RPR-030/031
- Guard: recipe-core 0620: G-24@1 SOURCE_ADDITION refused on GMP (EBMR-RPR-001)
- Guard: recipe-core 0624: whole-batch reprocessing planned only on a QCU-signed request citing a concluded deviation-lite investigation and a stored risk evaluation, product master permitting, not authorised by the issuer (EBMR-RPR-052, 053); recipe.reprocessing_release_problem reports a missing QC decision on additional testing
